                                           SCHEDULE B
                                DEVELOPMENT OF THE AIRPORT
                                           (See Clause 2.1)
 1. Development ofthe Airport
    ‘The planning approach to be used to falta the achievement ofa well-developed Airport should
    bye 10 establish alrside, terminal development and otber facility requirements on the basis   of
    orcasted future trafic for various Phases. Facility requirements should be the outcome ofthe
    application of technical and service level standards tothe relevant forecast data throughout the
    planing period to ensure tat sufficient favs are availableto accommodate       the forces traffic
    volumes from the COD ofthe Airport il the end ofthe Concession Peed
   ‘The most important systems that affect overall airport capacity and level of service to are the
   Runvay/ taxiway system and the passenger Terminal Building (andthe domestic and usersinterationl
   split as well a the number ofapron stands an contact gates. The development ofthe Aimpor in
   Phases shall be as per provisions mentioned in ScheduleA.
   In addition, regular investments are needed to periodically maiman and upgrade the facilis,
   replace equipment and mect ater requirement in order to ensure the Aion infastrcture cates
   to the projected atic meeting the service level sandards prescribed in this Agroment.
2. Development Philosophy.
    The purpose of any planning effort is to determine the facilities requited to support forecast
   demand, with the near-term planning being used as means of identifying        te initial development
   required. However, iti also imperative the planing effors do not initiate development that
   precludes or significantly complicates the ability 10 develop latter stages of the Airport.
   ‘Additionally, the planning effort must retin    a scheme that remainsexble while also definitly
  ‘establishing coordinate plan forthe incremental      grows of specific elements   ofthe Airport.
   Sustainabii

    ‘Safe movement
    In the operation, development and management of the Aiport, particular cre shall be taken to
   censure safety of Users & aircraft. For aircraft. the safety parameters are to be provided
    ‘ecordance with Civil Aviation Requirements (CAR). Provision of safety services like rescue
    ‘and firefighting, Apron Management, Wid life strike hazard reduction, Aerodrome Vehicle
    ‘operations, Aerodrome    Fencing and lighting tobe ensured. This shall inciude fii for safe
    ‘and efficient evacuation in ease ofemergency
    ‘System integrity
    In the design of power supply lighting. signaling, communication and security equipment,
    Paticular   cre shall be taken to minimize                    of failure. Preventive alnenance
                                                the likely incidence
    required to keep te services operational sal be ensured
    Restoration    of service
   ‘The Aieport shall be designed such that inthe event a fault occurs, 2 limited service can be
    provided within a few minutes by isolation ofthe affected area or equipment, 10 the extent
    posible. Secondary power supply requirements are to he provide in accordance with CAR.
